# Skills · A · part 166

1,012 entries, arxiv to arxiv-2608-06352v1-calibforge-adversarial-solver-calibratio…. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **arxiv**: Searches arXiv via the Atom REST API and enriches papers with Semantic Scholar citations, references, and BibTeX. Use when finding preprints by keyword, author, category, or ID, or generating citations. Not for Hugging Face paper pages (hugging-face-papers) or general web due-diligence (end-to-end-research). ([Kayforkind/skill-slice](https://github.com/Kayforkind/skill-slice/tree/HEAD/skills/arxiv))
- **arxiv**: Search arXiv, inspect paper metadata, and download papers by keyword or arXiv ID. Use when the user wants fresh arXiv search results, paper detail lookup, or direct PDF/source downloads. ([kenantang/codex-and-claude-skills](https://github.com/kenantang/codex-and-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/collected-academic-research-skills/sources/ymx10086__ResearchClaw/src/researchclaw/agents/skills/arxiv))
- **arxiv**: Search, download, and summarize academic papers from arXiv. ([KYRIE66nb/codex-omx-public-config](https://github.com/KYRIE66nb/codex-omx-public-config/tree/HEAD/home/.codex/skills/arxiv))
- **arxiv**: Search arXiv papers by keyword, author, category, or ID. ([nousresearch/hermes-agent](https://github.com/nousresearch/hermes-agent) · 754 installs)
- **arxiv**: Search arXiv by author, title, id, category, or date range; download a paper's PDF; or produce BibTeX / APA citations. Use for known-item and field lookups ("papers by Hinton", "cat:cs.LG since 2024", "cite 1706.03762", "download 2101.00001"). For open-ended topic discovery ("papers about X") prefer the alphaXiv discover_papers MCP tool instead. ([pd90506/cc-plugins](https://github.com/pd90506/cc-plugins/tree/HEAD/cc-arxiv/skills/arxiv))
- **arxiv**: Search arXiv papers by keyword, author, category, or ID; read abstracts and full papers; generate BibTeX entries; enrich with Semantic Scholar citation data, recommendations, and author profiles. Use when the user asks to find, read, cite, or analyze arXiv papers. ([plaesy/spec-kit](https://github.com/plaesy/spec-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/arxiv))
- **arxiv**: arXiv academic paper search and retrieval. Use when the agent needs this capability via MCP. ([RabbitAI-Lab/rabbit-plugins-mcp](https://github.com/RabbitAI-Lab/rabbit-plugins-mcp/tree/HEAD/plugins/arxiv/skills/arxiv))
- **ArXiv**: A working channel into arXiv: pull the newest preprints by subject, hunt by theme, or open a single paper by id or URL — with AlphaXiv machine briefs layered on where they exist. Covers cs.AI/cs.LG/cs.CL/cs.CR/cs.MA/cs.SE/cs.IR through the Latest, Search, and Paper flows. USE WHEN arxiv, papers, latest papers, research papers, recent ML papers, paper lookup, summarize paper, latest LLM papers, AI safety papers, cs.AI latest. NOT FOR broad web research (Research), raw URL parsing, or annual reports. ([ruban-s/DevOS](https://github.com/ruban-s/DevOS/tree/HEAD/skills/ArXiv))
- **arxiv**: Open-access preprint server for scientific papers (arxiv.org). Public Atom API at export.arxiv.org for search and metadata retrieval, plus HTML abstract pages. ([spike-faye-lei/spike-faye-lei-dsh-skills](https://github.com/spike-faye-lei/spike-faye-lei-dsh-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/research/arxiv))
- **arxiv**: Search arXiv papers by keyword, author, category, or ID. Use when finding, citing, or tracking academic papers and preprints. ([sunlianqiang/curated-skills-community](https://github.com/sunlianqiang/curated-skills-community/tree/HEAD/skills/arxiv))
- **arxiv**: This skill MUST be used for arXiv searches, arXiv URLs or paper IDs, and explicit requests to retrieve arXiv papers. It SHOULD be used as one source in general literature searches, preprint discovery, and related-work research when relevant to the topic. It MUST NOT treat arXiv as the only source for a general literature search. Triggers include arXiv、論文搜尋、預印本、文獻搜尋、相關研究。 ([TimLai666/skills](https://github.com/TimLai666/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/data-and-research/skills/arxiv))
- **arxiv**: Searches arXiv for academic papers by keyword, author, category, or ID. Load when the user needs to find recent research, track publications, or retrieve paper abstracts. ([unitalkai/unitalk-skills](https://github.com/unitalkai/unitalk-skills/tree/HEAD/web-and-research/arxiv))
- **arxiv**: Search, download, and summarize academic papers from arXiv. Use when user says "search arxiv", "download paper", "fetch arxiv", "arxiv search", "get paper pdf", or wants to find and save papers from arXiv to the local paper library. ([wanshuiyin/auto-claude-code-research-in-sleep](https://github.com/wanshuiyin/auto-claude-code-research-in-sleep) · 628 installs)
- **arxiv**: Search, download, and summarize academic papers from arXiv. Built for AI/ML researchers. Evolved from ractorrr/arxiv version 1.0.4 at 2026-05-15. ([whisolla/whistant-skills](https://github.com/whisolla/whistant-skills/tree/HEAD/tier-u/arxiv))
- **arxiv**: Use when a researcher searches or verifies arXiv papers. arxiv 既是托管检索源（search_papers source=arxiv）也是深读域（fetch_url 允许域内 /abs、/html）。 ([wyh2255/paper-research-assistant](https://github.com/wyh2255/paper-research-assistant/tree/HEAD/agentic-paper-search/agentic/skills/arxiv))
